_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
COLS = ["trade_id", "ts", "status", "stock", "side", "entry_price", "stop", "family", "weight",
        "risk_rupees", "result_R", "result_rupees", "cause", "engine"]

# The armed shadow each engine already writes its current PIT per-family weight into (weights from PRIOR
# fires only — point-in-time by construction), plus the JSON key that holds the {family: weight} map.
_SHADOW = {
    "E10": (os.path.join(_REPO, "roof", "store", "e10_weights_shadow.json"), "latest_weights"),
    "E11": (os.path.join(_REPO, "roof", "store", "e11_family_bias_shadow.json"), "latest_normal"),
}


def ledger(engine):
    return ACC.ENGINE_LEDGERS[str(engine).upper()]


def weight(engine, family):
    """This engine's live weight for a fire's family, from its armed shadow. COLD START = FLAT: no shadow,
    an unreadable one, or a family with no measured weight → 1.0 (never sizes up on a number it lacks)."""
    path, key = _SHADOW.get(str(engine).upper(), (None, None))
    if not path or not family:
        return 1.0
    try:
        w = json.load(open(path, encoding="utf-8")).get(key, {}).get(str(family))
        return float(w) if w is not None and float(w) > 0 else 1.0
    except Exception:
        return 1.0


def _read(engine):
    p = ledger(engine)
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return []
    try:
        return list(csv.DictReader(open(p, encoding="utf-8")))
    except Exception:
        return []


def _write(engine, rows):
    p = ledger(engine)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(p), exist_ok=True)
    with open(p, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=COLS)
        w.writeheader()
        for r in rows:
            w.writerow({c: r.get(c, "") for c in COLS})


def record_entry(engine, trade_id, stock, side, entry, stop, family="", *, ts="", event=True):
    """Book one engine's OPEN row for a fire the flat book just took, sized weight(family) × ₹1,000 on this
    engine's own risk unit. Idempotent on trade_id. Appends a roof 'open' event (event=False in tests).
    Returns {weight, risk_rupees} or None if already booked."""
    eng = str(engine).upper()
    rows = _read(eng)
    if any(r.get("trade_id") == str(trade_id) for r in rows):
        return None
    w = weight(eng, family)
    risk = round(w * ACC.R_RUPEES, 2)
    rows.append({"trade_id": str(trade_id), "ts": ts, "status": "open", "stock": stock, "side": side,
                 "entry_price": entry, "stop": stop, "family": family, "weight": round(w, 4),
                 "risk_rupees": risk, "result_R": "", "result_rupees": "", "cause": "", "engine": eng})
    _write(eng, rows)
    if event:
        _roof_event("open", eng, _roof_tid(trade_id, eng), stock=stock, side=side, entry=entry, stop=stop)
    return {"weight": w, "risk_rupees": risk}


def record_exit(engine, trade_id, result_R, cause="", *, ts="", event=True):
    """Book the engine's EXIT: result_rupees = result_R × THIS row's own risk (never ×₹1,000). Appends a
    roof 'exit' event carrying the cause. Returns the updated row or None."""
    eng = str(engine).upper()
    rows = _read(eng)
    for r in rows:
        if r.get("trade_id") == str(trade_id) and r.get("status") == "open":
            risk = float(r.get("risk_rupees") or ACC.R_RUPEES)
            r["status"] = "exited"
            r["result_R"] = round(float(result_R), 3)
            r["result_rupees"] = round(float(result_R) * risk, 2)
            r["cause"] = cause
            r["ts"] = ts or r.get("ts", "")
            _write(eng, rows)
            if event:
                _roof_event("exit", eng, _roof_tid(trade_id, eng), R=result_R, cause=cause)
            return r
    return None


def _roof_tid(trade_id, engine):
    """The roof keys copies by SOURCE trade id. E1/E10/E11 share one trade id per fire, so each engine's
    roof event carries an engine-distinct id ('<tid>#E10') — otherwise three sources on one fire collapse
    into one and the desk mis-books its N× hold. E1's own event keeps the plain tid; three distinct ids →
    the roof holds 3×, and each exits independently."""
    return "%s#%s" % (trade_id, engine)


def _roof_event(kind, engine, trade_id, **kw):
    try:
        from scripts import roof_copycat
        roof_copycat.append_event(kind, engine, trade_id, **kw)
    except Exception:
        pass

def account(name="desk"):
    """The named account's snapshot. 'desk' → the roof's copies over ₹2,00,000×live-engines; an engine or
    sizing-account name → that book over ₹2,00,000. Every ₹ reconciles to that account's ledger."""
    if str(name).lower() == "desk":
        return snapshot(rows=_desk_rows(), start=desk_start(), name="desk")
    return snapshot(rows=_engine_rows(name), start=ENGINE_START, name=str(name))

tool_result: def _candidates(asof=None, reader=None):
    """Daily-direction fires today (engine 1's own signal source), each with its walk-forward LB and horizon.
    Reuses the evening signal source; returns [] on any shortage (fail-safe)."""
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import evening
        reader = reader or (lambda t: ds.read(t, "raw"))
        cand = evening.active_daily_direction_signals()
        out = []
        for _, r in cand.iterrows():
            tok = int(r["stock"]); cond = r.get("condition"); metric = r.get("metric")
            if not isinstance(cond, str):
                continue
            fired, daily = evening._fire_today(reader, tok, metric, cond, asof)
            if not fired:
                continue
            out.append({"stock": tok, "direction": r["direction"], "p_lb": float(r["train_lb"]),
                        "sentence": cond, "daily": daily, "metric": metric})   # A4: family = metric
        return out
    except Exception:
        return []


def run_once(asof=None, reader=None, write=True, quotes=None):
    """One poll of the single order path. Returns a readout. Writes an order ONLY when gate.order_ok holds
    for it on this poll; under the R-HOLD-AND-FIX hold that is never, so this records the would-be book."""
    now_iso = clock.iso()
    streak = _live_loss_streak()
    cost_frac = money.COST_STATUTORY_RT + 2 * money.half_spread()
    cands = _candidates(asof, reader)
    by_stock = {c["stock"]: c for c in cands}
    # desk rules as CALLERS (R-HOLD-AND-FIX 2): net same-stock fires, and pause on the measured book guard.
    fired_orders = [{"stock": c["stock"], "bucket": "daily", "direction": c["direction"], "size": 1.0,
                     "strength": abs(c["p_lb"]), "sentence": c["sentence"]} for c in cands]
    netted = {int(o["stock"]): o for o in desk.net_orders(fired_orders)} if fired_orders else {}
    # R-BOOK20-COMPLETE (3): book guard (measured worst daily DD) + position cap (measured), both fail-closed.
    open_now = _open_position_count()
    open_keys = _open_pattern_keys()                   # R-FIX-3E (1): (instrument, pattern) already holding a position
    block_all = ("*", "*") in open_keys                # fail-closed sentinel: unreadable ledger → no new fires
    pos_cap = _measured_position_cap()
    cap_paused = open_now >= pos_cap
    guard_paused, guard_reason = desk.book_guard(_today_loss_rupees(), _measured_worst_daily_dd())
    would_be, written = [], []
    for stock, c in list(by_stock.items()):
        if stock not in netted:                       # netting cancelled this stock's fires -> no order
            continue
        c["direction"] = "direct" if netted[stock]["direction"] in ("direct", "long") else "inverse"
        tm = _typical_move(c["daily"]["close"] if hasattr(c["daily"], "columns") else c["daily"])
        if tm is None:
            continue
        ok, required = live_20d.required_move_ok(c["p_lb"], cost_frac, tm)
        if not ok:
            continue
        # moment light on the read-only quote (fail-closed offline -> not green -> record-only)
        inst = f"NSE:{c['stock']}"
        if quotes is not None:
            m_ok, _note = desk.moment_light(quotes.get(c["stock"]), key=inst)   # A1: per-stock ceiling
        else:
            m_ok, _note = live_20d.moment_light_live(inst)
        row = {"stock": c["stock"], "direction": c["direction"], "sentence": c["sentence"],
               "pattern": c.get("sentence", ""),      # R-FIX-3E (1): the open row carries its pattern for the re-fire key
               "required_move": round(required, 5), "typical_move": round(tm, 5),
               "moment_green": bool(m_ok), "horizon_days": H}
        would_be.append(row)
        # R-FIX-3E (1): NO RE-FIRE — never open a second position in a stock this pattern already holds.
        refire = block_all or (str(c["stock"]).strip(), str(c.get("sentence", "")).strip()) in open_keys
        allowed = (not guard_paused) and (not cap_paused) and (not refire) and gate.order_ok(
            engine=1, moment_green=(m_ok is True), live_loss_streak=streak)
        # R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per engine at fire time (PLACED / HELD), once per fire.
        _fire_cards(c, c.get("metric", ""), m_ok, bool(write and allowed), refire, cap_paused, guard_paused)
        if write and allowed:

tool_result: def _fire_cards(c, family, m_ok, placed, refire, cap_paused, guard_paused):
    """R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per ENGINE (E1/E10/E11) at fire time — this engine's own size and
    one outcome line (PLACED ₹… or HELD — <lock>). Sent ONCE per (day, stock, pattern, engine); never a
    repeat, never a demo (the notifier drops demo/test). Best-effort; never blocks the order path."""
    try:
        from scripts import portfolio_card as PC
        from scripts.notify import notify
        from engine1_rebuild import engine_book
        sym = _sym(c["stock"]); sentence = str(c.get("sentence", "") or "a certified same-stock pattern")
        direction = c.get("direction", "direct")
        day = clock.iso()[:10]
        seen = _load_seen()
        outcome_held = _fire_lock_reason(m_ok, refire, cap_paused, guard_paused)
        for eng in ("E1", "E10", "E11"):
            key = "%s|%s|%s|%s" % (day, sym, sentence, eng)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            w = 1.0 if eng == "E1" else engine_book.weight(eng, family)
            risk = round(w * money.PAPER_R_RUPEES, 2)
            outcome = ("PAPER ORDER PLACED %s" % PC.R(risk)) if placed else outcome_held
            try:
                notify(PC.fire_card("[%s]" % eng, sym, direction, sentence, risk, outcome))
                seen[key] = day
            except Exception:
                pass
        _save_seen(seen)
    except Exception:
        pass

tool_result: def _kite_span_fetch():
    """Default fetcher: the ONE-LOT SPAN margin for the near-month NIFTY/BANKNIFTY futures, from the Kite
    margins CALCULATOR (kiteconnect.basket_order_margins — READ-ONLY, it computes required margin and places
    NOTHING). Needs a live token (login time). Returns {instrument: {span, total, lot_size, tradingsymbol}}
    or {} on any failure — never raises, never places an order."""
    try:
        from kiteconnect import KiteConnect
        from data_acquisition import kite
        api_key = [redacted]("KITE_API_KEY")
        token = [redacted]()                       # today's access token (raises if absent)
        tok = token["access_token"] if isinstance(token, dict) else token
        kc = KiteConnect(api_key=[redacted])
        kc.set_access_token(tok)
        insts = [i for i in kc.instruments("NFO")
                 if i.get("segment") == "NFO-FUT" and i.get("name") in ("NIFTY", "BANKNIFTY")]
        out = {}
        for name in ("NIFTY", "BANKNIFTY"):
            fut = sorted((i for i in insts if i["name"] == name), key=lambda i: i["expiry"])
            if not fut:
                continue
            near = fut[0]                                # nearest expiry = near-month
            order = [{"exchange": "NFO", "tradingsymbol": near["tradingsymbol"], "transaction_type": "BUY",
                      "variety": "regular", "product": "NRML", "order_type": "MARKET",
                      "quantity": int(near["lot_size"])}]
            m = kc.basket_order_margins(order)
            span = float(m["orders"][0].get("span", 0)) if m.get("orders") else 0.0
            total = float(m.get("final", {}).get("total", 0)) or (
                float(m["orders"][0].get("total", 0)) if m.get("orders") else 0.0)
            out[name] = {"span": round(span, 2), "total": round(total, 2),
                         "lot_size": int(near["lot_size"]), "tradingsymbol": near["tradingsymbol"]}
        return out
    except Exception:
        return {}


def record_span(today=None, fetch=None):
    """R-NIGHT-CLOSE (3): at login, record the one-lot SPAN margin for NIFTY/BANKNIFTY from the Kite margins
    API and write reports/E21-SPAN.json. Sizing stays RECORD-ONLY until this file exists. Fail-safe: an empty
    fetch writes nothing (record-only continues). Read-only — nothing here places an order."""
    data = (fetch or _kite_span_fetch)()
    if not data:
        return {}
    from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
    stamp = (datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))).isoformat()
             if today is None else str(today))
    payload = {"recorded_at": stamp, "source": "kite basket_order_margins (read-only calc)", "margins": data}
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(SPAN_FILE), exist_ok=True)
    with open(SPAN_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(payload, f, indent=1)
    return payload


def recorded_span(name):
    """The recorded one-lot SPAN margin + lot size for `name`, or (None, None) if not yet recorded."""
    try:
        d = json.load(open(SPAN_FILE, encoding="utf-8"))["margins"][name]
        return float(d.get("total") or d.get("span")), int(d["lot_size"])
    except Exception:
        return None, None


def record_marks(today=None):
    """R-OVERNIGHT 'Live': from TOMORROW, capture the 15:25 and 09:20 near-month futures marks daily so the
    walk-forward scores on REAL fills. Appends one row per instrument per day to reports/E21-MARKS.csv from
    the day's own futures bars (read-only). Returns the rows written. Best-effort; the recorder loop calls it."""
    import csv
    from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
    ist = datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone(timezone(hours := timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))) if today is None \
        else pd.Timestamp(today)
    day = (ist.date() if hasattr(ist, "date") else ist).isoformat() if today is None else str(today)
    out = os.path.join(REPO, "reports", "E21-MARKS.csv")
    rows = []
    for name in INSTRUMENTS:
        try:
            fut = _fut(name)
            hm = fut.index.strftime("%H:%M"); dd = fut.index.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            entry = fut[(hm == ENTRY_HM) & (dd == day)]["close"]
            openb = fut[(hm == EXIT_HM) & (dd == day)]["open"]
            rows.append({"date": day, "instrument": name,
                         "close_1525": float(entry.iloc[-1]) if len(entry) else "",
                         "open_0920": float(openb.iloc[-1]) if len(openb) else ""})
        except Exception:
            continue
    if rows:
        new = not os.path.exists(out)
        with open(out, "a", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=["date", "instrument", "close_1525", "open_0920"])
            if new:
                w.writeheader()
            for r in rows:
                w.writerow(r)
    return rows

tool_result: def _caption_count(chan_dir):
    return len(glob.glob(os.path.join(chan_dir, "*.vtt"))) + len(glob.glob(os.path.join(chan_dir, "*.srt")))


def download_new(name, url, newest=40, timeout=900):
    """Download the newest `newest` videos' English auto-captions for this channel (skip the video itself),
    honouring the shared --download-archive so only NEW ones are pulled. Returns (before, after, added)."""
    chan_dir = os.path.join(OUT, _slug(name))
    os.makedirs(chan_dir, exist_ok=True)
    before = _caption_count(chan_dir)
    _run([YTDLP, "--download-archive", ARCHIVE, "--write-auto-subs", "--sub-langs", "en.*",
          "--skip-download", "--no-warnings", "--playlist-end", str(newest),
          "-o", os.path.join(chan_dir, "%(upload_date)s_%(id)s.%(ext)s"), url + "/videos"], timeout)
    after = _caption_count(chan_dir)
    return before, after, after - before


def _slug(name):
    return "".join(c if c.isalnum() else "_" for c in name)


def run(newest=40, write=True):
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    res = {"channels": {}, "resolved": 0, "unresolved": [], "substitutions": SUBSTITUTIONS}
    for name, url in CHANNELS.items():
        ok, n, note = resolve(url)
        entry = {"url": url, "resolves": ok, "videos_found": n, "note": note}
        if ok:
            res["resolved"] += 1
            b, a, added = download_new(name, url, newest=newest)
            entry.update({"captions_before": b, "captions_after": a, "captions_added": added})
        else:
            res["unresolved"].append(name)
        res["channels"][name] = entry
    if write:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(REPORT), exist_ok=True)
        with open(REPORT, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(res, f, indent=1)
    return res

tool_result: Permission to use Bash with command cat >> engine_youtube/download.py <<'PY'


def coverage(timeout=120):
    """R-NOW-22 item 3: per channel, compare the full video list to the downloaded caption files and report
    videos WITHOUT captions. Read-only (a --flat-playlist id list per channel + the .vtt files on disk).
    Returns {channel: {videos, with_captions, without_captions, missing_ids[]}}. Never fabricates."""
    import re as _re
    out = {}
    for name, url in CHANNELS.items():
        chan_dir = os.path.join(OUT, _slug(name))
        have = set()
        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(chan_dir, "*.vtt")) + glob.glob(os.path.join(chan_dir, "*.srt")):
            m = _re.search(r"_([0-9A-Za-z_-]{6,})\.(?:en[.-][^.]*|en)\.(?:vtt|srt)$", os.path.basename(p))
            if m:
                have.add(m.group(1))
        r = _run([YTDLP, "--flat-playlist", "--print", "id", "--no-warnings", url + "/videos"], timeout)
        ids = [ln.strip() for ln in (r.stdout or "").splitlines() if ln.strip()]
        missing = [i for i in ids if i not in have]
        out[name] = {"videos": len(ids), "with_captions": len(ids) - len(missing),
                     "without_captions": len(missing), "missing_ids": missing[:50],
                     "note": "no video list (unresolved/blocked)" if not ids else ""}
    try:
        with open(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(REPORT), "YOUTUBE-COVERAGE.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(out, f, indent=1)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return out
PY

            print(f"{_ist():%H:%M} site {'pushed' if changed else 'unchanged'}")
        except Exception as e:                      # a bad refresh must never end the keeper
            print(f"{_ist():%H:%M} site refresh error: {type(e).__name__}: {str(e)[:160]}")
        try:
            gap = IN_SESSION_SECS if live._in_session() else IDLE_SECS
        except Exception:
            gap = IDLE_SECS
        time.sleep(gap)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

tool_result: def build(snapshot=None):
    """R-E1-TERMINAL: the site is the static terminal in terminal/ plus the JSON it polls. The page
    functions above are the retired first site, kept for reference and no longer called."""
    import shutil
    import time
    from scripts import terminal_data, terminal_snapshot
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(SITE, "data"), exist_ok=True)
    for name in os.listdir(TERMINAL):
        shutil.copy2(os.path.join(TERMINAL, name), os.path.join(SITE, name))
    # R-EVENING-NOW (1, amendment 2): render the DELIVERABLES tab into index.html. Durable — the template
    # carries the tab button/panel + the __DELIVERABLES__ placeholder; the generator fills it (and writes
    # site/deliverables.xlsx that the button links to), so the loop keeps it. Best-effort.
    try:
        from scripts import deliverables_page as DP
        idx = os.path.join(SITE, "index.html")
        if os.path.exists(idx):
            t = open(idx, encoding="utf-8").read()
            if "__DELIVERABLES__" in t:
                open(idx, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(t.replace("__DELIVERABLES__", DP.deliverables_section()))
    except Exception:
        pass
    # R-PAPER-CAPITAL: current equity of the one persistent paper account, shown on every page. Machine-
    # derived from the ledger; site/data/account.json + reports/PAPER-ACCOUNT.md, and the home page's
    # __PAPER_EQUITY__ pill is filled in place. Best-effort; never fatal to a build.
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
        snap = ACC.account("desk")
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(SITE, "data"), exist_ok=True)
        summ = ACC.accounts_summary()
        with open(os.path.join(SITE, "data", "account.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump({"desk": snap, "accounts": summ}, f, indent=1, default=str)
        book = snap["equity"] - snap["start_equity"]
        acct_lines = "".join("- **%s:** %s\n" % (k, ACC.rupees(v)) for k, v in summ.items())
        with open(os.path.join(REPORTS, "PAPER-ACCOUNT.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("# PAPER ACCOUNTS — persistent books (R-PAPER-CAPITAL · R-ROOF-COPYCAT-NOW · "
                    "R-ADAPTIVE-SIZING-LIVE)\n\n"
                    "_Each account starts at %s, set once on %s; 1R = %s. Equity = starting + realised P&L "
                    "(fees inside), carried day to day, never reset or topped up without an owner ruling. "
                    "Engine 1 runs the SAME fires two ways — FLAT (1R=%s) and ADAPTIVE (a measured fraction "
                    "of equity); the desk (₹2,00,000 × live engines) holds the roof's copies. RECORD-ONLY._"
                    "\n\n## Every account, current equity\n\n%s\n"
                    "## Desk\n\n- **Current equity:** %s\n- **Realised P&L (all time):** %s\n"
                    "- **Open positions:** %d (risk deployed %s)\n- **Trades settled:** %d · win rate %s\n"
                    % (ACC.rupees(ACC.ENGINE_START), snap["start_date"], ACC.rupees(snap["r_rupees"]),
                       ACC.rupees(ACC.R_RUPEES), acct_lines,
                       ACC.rupees(snap["equity"]), ACC.rupees(book), snap["open_count"],
                       ACC.rupees(snap["risk_deployed_rupees"]), snap["trades"],
                       ("%.1f%%" % snap["win_rate"]) if snap["win_rate"] is not None else "—"))
        pill = "equity " + ACC.rupees(snap["equity"])
        for page in ("index.html", "engines.html", "results.html"):
            fp = os.path.join(SITE, page)
            if os.path.exists(fp):
                txt = open(fp, encoding="utf-8").read()
                if "__PAPER_EQUITY__" in txt:
                    open(fp, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(txt.replace("__PAPER_EQUITY__", html.escape(pill)))
    except Exception:
        pass
    for old, tab in OLD_PAGES.items():
        with open(os.path.join(SITE, old), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f'<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0; url=index.html#{tab}">'
                    f'<title>Moved</title><a href="index.html#{tab}">This page moved to the E1 Terminal.</a>')
    scr = os.path.join(SITE, "data", "screener.json")
    stale = not os.path.exists(scr) or time.time() - os.path.getmtime(scr) > SNAPSHOT_MAX_AGE_S
    if snapshot is None:
        try:
            from scripts.memory_guard import should_throttle
            snapshot = stale and not should_throttle()
        except Exception:
            snapshot = stale
    if snapshot:
        terminal_snapshot.build()
    terminal_data.build()
    # R-E1-FACTS-PAGE: refresh the daily 323-facts page (site/facts.html + reports/FACTS-PAGE.md)
    try:
        from scripts import facts_page
        facts_page.build()
    except Exception:
        pass
    # R-DATA-LANES: refresh the data-readiness dashboard (site/data-readiness.html + reports/DATA-READINESS.md)
    try:
        from scripts import data_lanes, graveyard, allocator, roof_scoreboard, engines_page, results_page
        data_lanes.build(); graveyard.build(); allocator.build(); roof_scoreboard.build()
        engines_page.build(); results_page.build()     # R-RESULTS-PAGE: results.html + per-test blotters
        from scripts import deliverables_page, messages_page, recording_page
        deliverables_page.build()                       # R-EVENING-NOW (1): site/deliverables.xlsx (all calls)
        messages_page.build()                           # R-EVENING-NOW amend 3: site/data/messages.json (30d)
        recording_page.build()                          # R-EVENING-NOW amend 4: site/data/recording.json (lanes)
        from scripts import engine_page
        engine_page.build()                             # R-DEEP-DIVE: site/engine-e1.html (deep dive)
        from scripts import site_v2
        site_v2.build()                                # R-NIGHT-2 Part B: site/v2/ preview
        try:                                           # R-MORNING-22 item 4: capture today's E21 15:25/09:20
            from engine21_overnight import overnight as _E21   # marks through the day (idempotent, read-only)
            _E21.record_marks()
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:                                           # R-MORNING-22 item 2: run each fixed-rule engine's own
            from scripts import engine_signals as _ES  # signal, guarded + idempotent per day; diary-only engines
            _ES.run_all()                              # skip themselves, a failing engine never stops the others
        except Exception:
            pass
        from scripts import uncle_page
        uncle_page.build()                              # R-UNCLE-PAGE: site/for-nikhil.html, plain trader language
        for mod, call in (("sizing_test", "build"),     # R-SIZING-TEST: refresh the sizing diaries + report
                          ("ask_the_desk", "build"),    # R-ASK-THE-DESK: site/ask-the-desk.html
                          ("stock_profiles", "build"),  # R-DEPTH (3): site/stocks/<symbol>.html for all 210
                          ("e19_finder", "run")):        # R-DEPTH (2): E19 finder ledger + report (lane-gated)
            try:
                getattr(__import__("scripts." + mod, fromlist=[mod]), call)()
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception:
        pass
    for f in ("E1-BLOTTER.csv", "E1-BLOTTER.xlsx", "E1-FIRE-DIARY.csv", "FACTS-PAGE.md",
              "E1-TWO-WEIGHTS.md", "E4-LIVE-RECORD.md", "E11-FAMILY-BIAS.md", "E3-DATES-NSE.md",
              "E3-DATES.md", "REGIME-PERSISTENCE.md", "E9-PAIRS.md", "E11-VOL-SIZING.md", "SLOW-TREND.md", "SEASON-RULER.md", "SHAPE-REGISTRY.md", "OPEN-ITEMS.md", "E12-XCHANGE.md", "TIME-EFFECTS.md", "SHAPES-210.md", "POSITIONING.md", "BROKER-SURVEY.md",
              "AUDIT-3.md", "ROOF-SCOREBOARD.md", "DATA-READINESS.md", "GRAVEYARD-DIARY.md",
              "LANE14-PUBLIC-FLOWS.md", "LANE15-MACRO.md", "LANE16-BREADTH.md", "E11-INDEX-CHANGE.md",
              "LANE-BACKTEST.md", "ROOF-COMBINED-LEDGER.csv", "RESULTS.md",
              "INDEX-TESTS.md", "E1-LIVE-GATE-1.md", "E19-FINDER.md", "RULER-FACTORY.md",
              "PAPER-ACCOUNT.md", "E3-CALENDAR-DAILY.md"):
        src = os.path.join(REPORTS, f)
        if os.path.exists(src):
            with open(src, "rb") as a, open(os.path.join(SITE, f), "wb") as b:
                b.write(a.read())
    return SITE


if __name__ == "__main__":
    out = build()
    print("SITE built:", out, "->", ", ".join(sorted(os.listdir(out))))

REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
IN_SESSION_SECS, IDLE_SECS = 60, 300      # R-E1-TERMINAL: 60 s in session, 5 min otherwise


def _ist():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)))


def _git(*args, timeout=120):
    from scripts import quiet
    return quiet.run(["git", *args], cwd=REPO, capture_output=True, text=True,
                     timeout=timeout, check=False)


_DIGEST_DAY = None
_PREOPEN_DAY = None
_SUMMARY_DAY = None


def _telegram_day_reports():
    """R-E1-TELEGRAM-STYLE A4: fire the 09:00 pre-open list and the 15:40 day summary once each per
    trading day (informational). Uses the trading calendar so it stays quiet on holidays/weekends."""
    global _PREOPEN_DAY, _SUMMARY_DAY
    now = _ist()
    today = now.date().isoformat()
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import trading_calendar as CAL
        if not CAL.is_trading_day_or_weekday(now.date())[0]:
            return
    except Exception:
        if now.weekday() >= 5:
            return
    from scripts import day_report
    if (now.hour, now.minute) >= (9, 0) and (now.hour, now.minute) < (15, 40) and _PREOPEN_DAY != today:
        _PREOPEN_DAY = today
        try:
            day_report.preopen()
        except Exception:
            pass
    if (now.hour, now.minute) >= (15, 40) and _SUMMARY_DAY != today:
        _SUMMARY_DAY = today
        try:
            day_report.summary()
        except Exception:
            pass


def _daily_digest_due():
    """R-E1-LEAD-DIGEST: regenerate the lead digest once a day at/after 15:40 IST (the site loop's
    half of 'after every report and at 15:40 daily'; a report also regenerates it directly)."""
    global _DIGEST_DAY
    now = _ist()
    today = now.date().isoformat()
    if (now.hour, now.minute) >= (15, 40) and _DIGEST_DAY != today:
        _DIGEST_DAY = today
        return True
    return False


def once():
    from scripts import site_build, publish_site, lead_digest, snapshot_sync
    site_build.build()
    _daily_digest_due()                       # keeps the 15:40 one-per-day marker; digest builds every cycle
    lead_digest.build()                       # E1-STATUS-3: refresh the digest each cycle so it is never stale
    _telegram_day_reports()                   # R-E1-TELEGRAM-STYLE A4: 09:00 pre-open, 15:40 day summary
    publish_site.publish(f"E1 live site {_ist():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} IST")   # public Pages, after every build
    # R-RESTART-RESUME (3): the repo commit happens ONLY in the separate snapshot folder (the builder's tree is
    # only read), and it PAUSES while a build/audit session holds the repo. The site build and the public-Pages
    # publish above keep running — they touch no git lock in this repo (Pages pushes from its own clone).
    # Also carries the evening ledger + the scan's pattern board. Push failure: backoff, then next cycle.
    return snapshot_sync.sync(f"E1 site refresh {_ist():%Y-%m-%d %H:%M} IST") == "pushed"

def post_login():
    """Runs once a fresh access token is on disk (from any catcher/gate): verify → top-up → arm the
    evening cycle → the plain [E1] line. Guarded; any failure sends ONE 🙋 and spends nothing.
    Reusable by scripts/kite_catcher.py (the port-80 auto-catcher) and kite_login.main()."""
    # R-TOKEN-DIAG: _verify() classifies + 🙋s on failure and returns False; on success recorders start now.
    if not _verify():
        return
    _arm_recorder()          # R-HIDE-WINDOWS: recorders start the MOMENT the test call succeeds (hidden)
    # top-up 2026-07-15 -> today (read-only historical fetch), then merge + adjust
    try:
        kite.fetch_slices(_minute_specs(TODAY_ISO))
        done = _merge_topup_into_store(TODAY_ISO)
        from engine1_rebuild import adjuster as ADJ, datastore as ds
        for tok in ds.frozen_tokens():
            try:
                ADJ.adjust_token(tok, "raw")   # new adjusted copy; never overwrites raw
            except Exception:
                pass
    except Exception as e:
        notify(f"\U0001f64b [E1] Kite top-up stopped ({type(e).__name__}) — nothing was paid. "
               f"Do: check Kite credits/subscription on the laptop, then re-run START_E1_KITE.bat.")
        return
    notify(f"[E1] Kite logged in. Top-up done for {done} of 210 instruments (2026-07-15 → {TODAY_ISO}, "
           f"adjusted, tagged kite_topup). Recorders already running. Evening live run armed after 15:30 IST.")
    _arm_book20()          # R-KITE-LOGIN-FIX / R-HOLD-AND-FIX: the single order path (never the retired loop)
    _arm_evening()
    try:                   # R-NIGHT-CLOSE (3): record the E21 one-lot SPAN margin (read-only calc); sizing
        from engine21_overnight import overnight as E21   # stays record-only until this succeeds
        overnight_span = E21.record_span()
        if overnight_span:
            E21.record_marks()                            # and today's 15:25/09:20 marks for live scoring
    except Exception:
        pass


def _arm_book20():
    """R-KITE-LOGIN-FIX (3) + R-HOLD-AND-FIX (2): start the 20-day BOOK runner (the ONLY order path)
    detached — but ONLY if the R-HOLD-AND-FIX hold is lifted. While held, start nothing and say so. The
    retired intraday loop (run_live/live/live_engine) is NEVER started here."""
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import gate
        if gate.held():
            notify("[E1] 20-day book is HELD (R-HOLD-AND-FIX: no orders until AUDIT-3B passes) — the order "
                   "runner was NOT started. Recorders are running; the book records only. The retired "
                   "intraday loop is not started.")
            return
        import subprocess
        repo = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
        flags = 0x08000000 if os.name == "nt" else 0     # CREATE_NO_WINDOW
        subprocess.Popen([_pythonw(), "-m", "engine1_rebuild.book20_runner", "--loop"],
                         cwd=repo, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL, creationflags=flags)
        notify("[E1] 20-day book runner started (the single order path; every poll re-checks the fail-closed "
               "gate stack). Paper only; no broker order call.")
    except Exception:
        pass


def _pythonw():
    """R-HIDE-WINDOWS: the windowless interpreter (pythonw.exe) so a launched job NEVER pops a console."""
    exe = sys.executable
    cand = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(exe), "pythonw.exe")
    return cand if os.path.exists(cand) else exe


def _arm_recorder():

    hidden, free RAM logged, never started twice. Then send exactly ONE line 'recorders: N/5 running' (this

        line = "[E1] recorders: %d/%d running" % (up, total)
        if down:
            line += " — down: " + ", ".join(down)
        notify(line)
    except Exception:

def have_login(today=None):
    from data_acquisition import kite
    try:
        kite.load_token(today=today); return True

        return False


def _universe():
    # R-DAY1-BUNDLE A1 (+ its 204-name addendum): record the five-level DEPTH of EVERY watched F&O CASH stock
    # as "NSE:<symbol>", not just the 95 that happen to keep a futures file on disk. Every watched name has a
    # cash order book with five bid/ask levels (verified live). The desk watches the frozen universe
    # (render.symbol over datastore.frozen_tokens — all 210 have clean NSE symbols), so all watched names are
    # covered, per stock. NO API LIMIT prevents this: Kite's /quote endpoint accepts up to ~500 instruments
    # per call (kite.fetch_quote docstring) and this recorder batches at BATCH=200, so ~210 names fit in two
    # calls. (Indices and the continuous "-I" future keys are gone — they carry no order book / are not
    # tradeable keys, which is why the old universe wrote zero snapshots.)
    from engine1_rebuild import render
    return ["NSE:" + render.symbol(t) for t in ds.frozen_tokens()]


def _rows_from_quote(qmap):
    """Flatten five-level depth to rows: key, level, side, price, qty. Missing depth -> skipped (fail-closed)."""
    ts = datetime.now(IST).isoformat()
    out = []
    for key, q in qmap.items():
        depth = (q or {}).get("depth") or {}
        for side in ("buy", "sell"):
            for lvl, e in enumerate((depth.get(side) or [])[:5], 1):
                if e.get("price"):
                    out.append({"ts": ts, "key": key, "side": side, "level": lvl,
                                "price": e.get("price"), "qty": e.get("quantity")})
    return out


def record_once(today=None):
    if not have_login(today=today):

    from data_acquisition import kite
    keys = _universe()
    snap = {}
    for i in range(0, len(keys), BATCH):
        try:
            snap.update(kite.fetch_quote(keys[i:i + BATCH], today=today))
        except Exception:
=== run_market_loop ===
def run_market_loop(max_minutes=None, today=None, _sleep=None):
    import time
    _sleep = _sleep or time.sleep
    n = 0
    from scripts.recorder_manifest import session_start
    session_start(STORE, "E13 five-level depth", logged_in=have_login(today=today), today=today)   # R-FIX-3E (4)
    while have_login(today=today):
        now = datetime.now(IST); hm = now.hour * 60 + now.minute
        if now.weekday() >= 5 or not (9*60+15 <= hm <= 15*60+30):
            break
        record_once(today=today); n += 1
        if max_minutes and n >= max_minutes:
            break
        _sleep(60)
    return n


def _demo():

    assert record_once(today="1900-01-01") == 0                        # no token -> fail closed
    print("e13_depth demo OK: five-level flatten (10 rows), fail-closed; universe=%d, days=%d, 5L present=%s"
          % (len(_universe()), days_recorded(), five_level_present()))

tool_result: def running_cmdlines():
    """Command lines of every python process. [] if unreadable (then a duplicate is possible, never a kill)."""
    if os.name != "nt":
        return []
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command",
                            "Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name like 'python%'\" | "
                            "ForEach-Object { $_.CommandLine }"],
                           capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=60, creationflags=NO_WINDOW)
        return [l.strip() for l in r.stdout.splitlines() if l.strip()]
    except Exception:
        return []

=== start() ===
def start(label, args, cmdlines=None, _popen=subprocess.Popen):
    """Start one job hidden unless it is already running. Logs free RAM with the decision. Returns bool."""
    cmdlines = running_cmdlines() if cmdlines is None else cmdlines
    if is_running(args, cmdlines):
        MG.log_launch(label, "already running - left alone")
        return False
    MG.log_launch(label, "START (hidden, pythonw)")
    _popen([pythonw(), *args], cwd=REPO, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
           creationflags=NO_WINDOW | DETACHED)
    return True


def recorders_running(cmdlines=None):
    """R-DAY1-BUNDLE A2: how many of the 5 recording lanes (E4/E7/E12/E13/E14) are actually up now, and the
    labels of any that are down. Returns (n_up, total, down_labels). Fail-safe: an unreadable process list
    yields (0, 5, all) so the login line never over-reports."""
    cmdlines = running_cmdlines() if cmdlines is None else cmdlines
    up, down = 0, []
    for label, args in RECORDERS:
        if cmdlines and is_running(args, cmdlines):
            up += 1
        else:
            down.append(label)
    return up, len(RECORDERS), down

tool_result: def run_session(today=None):
    """Connect the read-only KiteTicker, subscribe to the eight-equation instrument set, record tick gaps +
    birth/death, ping latency; store episodes for the day. Token-gated + fail-closed. (The live socket loop
    runs only on a login day; the gap math + episode tracking are unit-tested offline via _demo.)"""
    from scripts.recorder_manifest import session_start
    session_start(STORE, "E7 arbitrage-tick", logged_in=have_login(today=today), today=today)   # R-FIX-3E (4)
    if not have_login(today=today):
        return 0
    # (on a login day: from kiteconnect import KiteTicker; subscribe to the parity CE/PE/FUT ladders, the
    # spot/near/far futures for basis+calendar, the box/butterfly strike ladders, the index + constituents
    # for index-vs-basket, and the NSE+BSE pair; on_ticks -> update the latest prices, compute each
    # equation's net gap, feed the per-(equation,instrument) Episode, append completed episodes with their
    # ms lifetime + the latency ping to today's parquet + manifest. Read-only socket; no order call.)
    return 0


def _demo():
    # parity gap: rich call vs put widens the residual; net = |raw| - cost
    net, raw = parity_gap(120, 80, 20000, 20000, 7, money.COST_RT)
    assert abs(raw - ((120 - 80) - (20000 - 20000 * (2.718281828 ** (-RF*7/365))))) < 1e-6
    # episode birth/death lifetime
    ep = Episode()
    assert ep.update(5.0, 1000) is None and ep.birth_ms == 1000          # born
    assert ep.update(3.0, 1400) is None                                  # still alive
    life = ep.update(-1.0, 1600); assert life == 600                     # died after 600 ms
    b, r = basis_gap(20000, 20050, 30, money.COST_RT)
    assert isinstance(b, float)
    assert run_session(today="1900-01-01") == 0                          # no token -> fail closed
    print("tick_recorder demo OK: parity/basis gap-after-cost, episode birth/death ms, fail-closed; "
          "equations=%d, sessions=%d" % (len(EQUATIONS), sessions_recorded()))

tool_use Bash {"command": "sed -n '82,135p' scripts/notify.py", "description": "Read notify demo-drop logic"}
tool_result: def _demo_context(message):
    """R-NO-DEMO-TELEGRAM: True if this send comes from a demo/test/dry-run context, or carries a
    demo/synthetic marker — such a message must NEVER reach Telegram. Checks: --demo/--test in argv; a
    _demo / test_ / pytest frame anywhere on the call stack; or a 'demo'/'DEMOKEY'/'SIM-' marker or the
    synthetic instrument id 111 in the message."""
    import sys as _sys
    import inspect as _inspect
    if any(a in ("--demo", "--test") for a in _sys.argv):
        return "argv --demo/--test"
    try:
        for fr in _inspect.stack():
            fn = fr.function
            fp = (fr.filename or "").replace("\\", "/")
            if fn == "_demo" or fn.startswith("test_") or "/tests/" in fp or "pytest" in fp or "site-packages/_pytest" in fp:
                return "call stack: %s (%s)" % (fn, os.path.basename(fp))
    except Exception:
        pass
    m = str(message)
    for mark in ("DEMOKEY", "SIM-", " demo", "demo ", "(demo)", "· 111 ·", " 111 "):
        if mark in m or mark.lower() in m.lower():
            return "message marker %r" % mark
    return None


def _drop_demo(message, why):
    """Log the dropped demo/test message; never send it."""
    try:
        path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))), "reports", "NOTIFY-DROPPED.log")
        with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("DROPPED (%s): %s\n" % (why, str(message)[:200]))
    except Exception:
        pass


# STANDING RULE (R-MORNING-FIX / R-DAY1-BUNDLE A6 / R-AUDIT-DAY1-FIX, 2026-09-21): NO Telegram message may
# repeat on a timer. A 🙋 (or any line) fires ONCE PER CAUSE and never again until the cause changes. Any
# caller that lives in a loop must gate its send on a state change or a rate-limit keyed to the event (see
# portfolio_card._changed, watchdog.rescan_check / check_once) — never send blindly every N minutes.
# THE TRUTH (corrected after AUDIT-DAY1): the "Builder idle" sender was deleted (watchdog.builder_check now
# returns False). The "token is missing" sender was deleted from engine1_rebuild.live (on a missing token
# live.run() returns fail-closed SILENTLY) — but that was not enough on its own, because run_live kept being
# LAUNCHED (launch_e1.ESSENTIAL) and RESURRECTED (watchdog._expected_jobs read a REM-commented run_live line),
# so an OLD cached run_live re-emitted the line every ~120s. R-AUDIT-DAY1-FIX RETIRED run_live: it is removed
# from launch_e1.ESSENTIAL and never resurrected (watchdog.RETIRED), so live.run() is no longer invoked and
# token_ping owns the login ask (the 🙋 login link, once per cause). Do not reintroduce a periodic sender.

    """

    if _why:

def test_execute_is_fail_closed_under_the_hold():
    from engine1_rebuild import gate
    orders = EX.desk_book([{"engine": "E1", "instrument": "NSE:REL", "side": "buy", "size": 2,
                            "stop": 2, "hold": 20}])["orders"]
    green = {"NSE:REL": {"depth": {"buy": [{"price": 99.9}], "sell": [{"price": 100.1}]}, "last_price": 100.0}}
    ex = EX.execute(orders, green, write=True)
    assert gate.held() is True
    assert ex["written"] == [] and len(ex["would_be"]) == 1

# sealed walk-forward, so the traded count is gated at a small declared floor and the LUCK CORRECTION (a
# Wilson lower bound at the variant-count-adjusted z) is the real bar — for n this small it only clears on a
# near-perfect record, which is the conservative behaviour we want. MIN_FIRES still gates the PRIOR evidence.
MIN_TRADES = 8
STAGING_JSON = os.path.join(ds._REPO, "reports", "EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.json")
STAGING_MD = os.path.join(ds._REPO, "reports", "EXAMS-REAL-STAGING.md")

# The grid = every (horizon, stop, trail). V = its size -> the luck correction is Bonferroni across V.
GRID = [(H, s, t) for H in HORIZONS for s in STOPS for t in TRAIL]
V = len(GRID)
_Z_LUCK = statistics.NormalDist().inv_cdf(1 - 0.05 / (2 * V))     # variant-count-adjusted two-sided z

def _in_window(now):
    o = now.replace(hour=OPEN[0], minute=OPEN[1], second=0, microsecond=0)
    c = now.replace(hour=CLOSE[0], minute=CLOSE[1], second=0, microsecond=0)
    return o <= now <= c


def _changed(text):
    """True if this card differs from the last one sent (suppress-if-unchanged). The stored digest omits
    the timestamp line so a pure clock tick never counts as a change."""
    body = "\n".join(l for l in text.splitlines() if not l.startswith("📊 PORTFOLIO"))
    h = hashlib.sha1(body.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    try:
        prev = open(STATE, encoding="utf-8").read().strip() if os.path.exists(STATE) else ""
    except Exception:
        prev = ""
    if h == prev:
        return False
    try:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(STATE), exist_ok=True)
        open(STATE, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(h)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return True


def tick(now=None, send=True):
    """One 30-minute beat: build the portfolio card, send it only if the market is open AND it changed.
    Returns the card text (whether or not it was sent) or None outside market hours."""
    now = now or _ist()
    trading = True
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import trading_calendar as CAL
        trading, _ = CAL.is_trading_day_or_weekday(now.date())
    except Exception:
        pass
    if not trading or not _in_window(now):
        return None
    try:                                              # R-FIX-3G (4): replay the engine event log into the desk
        from scripts import roof_copycat
        roof_copycat.replay(write=True)
    except Exception:
        pass
    card = portfolio_card(when=now.strftime("%H:%M"))
    if send and _changed(card):
        try:
            from scripts.notify import notify

def test_suppress_if_unchanged(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(PC, "STATE", str(tmp_path / "s"))
    live = {"recorders": (0, 5), "depth": "🔴", "breaker": "OK", "kill": "live", "gate": "HELD"}
    a = PC.portfolio_card(snap=_empty(), live=live, when="12:00")
    b = PC.portfolio_card(snap=_empty(), live=live, when="12:30")   # only the clock moved
    assert PC._changed(a) is True and PC._changed(b) is False       # a clock tick is not a change


def test_out_of_hours_tick_is_silent():
    from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
    ist = timezone(timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30))
    assert PC.tick(now=datetime(2026, 9, 21, 3, 0, tzinfo=ist), send=False) is None

tool_result: def portfolio_card(snap=None, live=None, when=None):
    snap = ACC.snapshot() if snap is None else snap
    live = live_state() if live is None else live
    when = when or _ist().strftime("%H:%M")
    run, tot = live["recorders"]
    if snap["open_positions"]:
        opens = "; ".join(
            "%s %s %s (%s)" % (o.get("engine", "E1"), (o.get("direction") or "").upper()[:1] or "?",
                               o["stock"], R(o["uPnL"]) if o["uPnL"] is not None else "u—")
            for o in snap["open_positions"])
    else:
        opens = "none"
    try:
        accts = ACC.accounts_summary()
        acct_line = " · ".join("%s %s" % (k, R(v)) for k, v in accts.items() if k != "desk")
        acct_line = ("\naccounts: %s · desk %s" % (acct_line, R(accts.get("desk", snap["equity"])))) if accts else ""
    except Exception:
        acct_line = ""
    return ("📊 PORTFOLIO %s · desk capital %s · deployed %s · cash %s · unrealized %s · realized %s · "
            "trades %dW/%dL · fills %d/%d · breaker %s · orders %s%s · depth %s · recorders %d/%d\n"
            "open: %s · worst case if all SLs hit %s%s"
            % (when, R(snap["equity"]), R(snap["risk_deployed_rupees"]), R(snap["cash_rupees"]),
               R(snap["unrealised_rupees"]), R(snap["realised_rupees"]), snap["wins"], snap["losses"],
               live.get("fills", 0), live.get("attempts", 0), live.get("breaker", "?"),
               live.get("gate", "?"), " · KILLED" if live.get("kill") == "KILLED" else "",
               live["depth"], run, tot, opens, R(snap["worst_case_rupees"]), acct_line))


def _inverse_pnl():
    """The E17 INVERSE sizing diary's running P&L in ₹, or None if that blotter is not on disk yet."""
    p = os.path.join(ds._REPO, "reports", "SIZING-E17-INVERSE-BLOTTER.csv")
    if not os.path.exists(p):
        return None
    try:
        import csv
        tot = 0.0
        for row in csv.DictReader(open(p, encoding="utf-8")):
            for k in ("result_rupees", "pnl_rupees", "net_rupees"):
                if row.get(k) not in (None, ""):
                    tot += float(row[k]); break
        return tot

tool_result: def _fire_cards(c, family, m_ok, placed, refire, cap_paused, guard_paused):
    """R-DAY1-BUNDLE A5: one card per fire per ENGINE (E1/E10/E11) at fire time — this engine's own size and
    one outcome line (PLACED ₹… or HELD — <lock>). Sent ONCE per (day, stock, pattern, engine); never a
    repeat, never a demo (the notifier drops demo/test). Best-effort; never blocks the order path."""
    try:
        from scripts import portfolio_card as PC
        from scripts.notify import notify
        from engine1_rebuild import engine_book
        sym = _sym(c["stock"]); sentence = str(c.get("sentence", "") or "a certified same-stock pattern")
        if c.get("management"):                            # R-MORNING-22 item 1: survivor cards carry the management
            sentence = "%s — %s" % (sentence, c.get("management"))
        direction = c.get("direction", "direct")
        day = clock.iso()[:10]
        seen = _load_seen()
        outcome_held = _fire_lock_reason(m_ok, refire, cap_paused, guard_paused)
        for eng in ("E1", "E10", "E11"):
            key = "%s|%s|%s|%s" % (day, sym, sentence, eng)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            w = 1.0 if eng == "E1" else engine_book.weight(eng, family)
            risk = round(w * money.PAPER_R_RUPEES, 2)
            outcome = ("PAPER ORDER PLACED %s" % PC.R(risk)) if placed else outcome_held
            try:
                notify(PC.fire_card("[%s]" % eng, sym, direction, sentence, risk, outcome))
                seen[key] = day
            except Exception:
                pass
        _save_seen(seen)

It looks for a stock being quietly accumulated: heavy trading AND\nheavy DELIVERY (shares actually taken, not intraday churn), a delivery share above its own normal and rising,\na quiet (narrow) day, and a price that held or rose. Long only, entry the next open, on paper with real costs, from the first day.\n\nMatch on day D (all versus the stock's own trailing 21 sessions, point-in-time):\n  1. volume in its top band          (today >= its 21-day 80th-percentile volume)\n  2. delivered quantity in its top band (today >= its 21-day 80th-percentile delivered qty)\n  3. delivery % above its median AND above the prior day\n  4. daily range below its median     ((high-low)/close < its 21-day median range)\n  5. price flat or up on the day      (close >= prev close)\nEntry: next session's open, long. Exit: the match is absent two sessions in a row, OR 10 sessions held, OR a\nstop at twice the stock's own daily swing (its 21-day median range) below entry. Cost: the calibrated round\ntrip charged on entry and exit. tool_result: def build_live():
    """LIGHT cycle (SITE-CYCLE split, ~every 2 min): refresh ONLY the live data the Live desk tab polls — the
    terminal shell, the live feed (positions / cards / ticker → data/live.json), and the paper accounts
    (data/account.json) — plus the cheap, time-sensitive engine steps (E21 marks, each engine's own fire).
    NO heavy page builders. Measured ~1 s, so the Live desk is current within the light interval."""
    import shutil
    from scripts import terminal_data
    os.makedirs(os.path.join(SITE, "data"), exist_ok=True)
    for name in os.listdir(TERMINAL):
        shutil.copy2(os.path.join(TERMINAL, name), os.path.join(SITE, name))
    _fill_deliverables()
    _write_accounts()
    _old_page_redirects()
    terminal_data.build()                               # the live feed the Live desk tab polls (~0.2 s)
    return SITE


def _fill_deliverables():
    try:
        from scripts import deliverables_page as DP
        idx = os.path.join(SITE, "index.html")
        if os.path.exists(idx):
            t = open(idx, encoding="utf-8").read()
            if "__DELIVERABLES__" in t:
                open(idx, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(t.replace("__DELIVERABLES__", DP.deliverables_section()))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _write_accounts():
    try:
        from engine1_rebuild import paper_account as ACC
        snap = ACC.account("desk")
        os.makedirs(os.path.join(SITE, "data"), exist_ok=True)
        summ = ACC.accounts_summary()
        with open(os.path.join(SITE, "data", "account.json"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump({"desk": snap, "accounts": summ}, f, indent=1, default=str)
        book = snap["equity"] - snap["start_equity"]
        acct_lines = "".join("- **%s:** %s\n" % (k, ACC.rupees(v)) for k, v in summ.items())
        with open(os.path.join(REPORTS, "PAPER-ACCOUNT.md"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("# PAPER ACCOUNTS — persistent books (R-PAPER-CAPITAL · R-ROOF-COPYCAT-NOW · "
                    "R-ADAPTIVE-SIZING-LIVE)\n\n"
                    "_Each account starts at %s, set once on %s; 1R = %s. Equity = starting + realised P&L "
                    "(fees inside), carried day to day, never reset or topped up without an owner ruling. "
                    "Engine 1 runs the SAME fires two ways — FLAT (1R=%s) and ADAPTIVE (a measured fraction "
                    "of equity); the desk (₹2,00,000 × live engines) holds the roof's copies. RECORD-ONLY._"
                    "\n\n## Every account, current equity\n\n%s\n"
                    "## Desk\n\n- **Current equity:** %s\n- **Realised P&L (all time):** %s\n"
                    "- **Open positions:** %d (risk deployed %s)\n- **Trades settled:** %d · win rate %s\n"
                    % (ACC.rupees(ACC.ENGINE_START), snap["start_date"], ACC.rupees(snap["r_rupees"]),
                       ACC.rupees(ACC.R_RUPEES), acct_lines,
                       ACC.rupees(snap["equity"]), ACC.rupees(book), snap["open_count"],
                       ACC.rupees(snap["risk_deployed_rupees"]), snap["trades"],
                       ("%.1f%%" % snap["win_rate"]) if snap["win_rate"] is not None else "—"))
        pill = "equity " + ACC.rupees(snap["equity"])
        for page in ("index.html", "engines.html", "results.html"):
            fp = os.path.join(SITE, page)
            if os.path.exists(fp):
                txt = open(fp, encoding="utf-8").read()
                if "__PAPER_EQUITY__" in txt:
                    open(fp, "w", encoding="utf-8").write(txt.replace("__PAPER_EQUITY__", html.escape(pill)))
    except Exception:
        pass


def _old_page_redirects():
    for old, tab in OLD_PAGES.items():
        with open(os.path.join(SITE, old), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f'<!doctype html><meta charset="utf-8"><meta http-equiv="refresh" content="0; url=index.html#{tab}">'
                    f'<title>Moved</title><a href="index.html#{tab}">This page moved to the E1 Terminal.</a>')

    """Print the 08:30 line from the last gate run (or run the gate if none)."""

def post(notifier=None):
    """Run the gate and POST the 08:30 line (one Telegram line). Returns the line. The owner's 08:15 bat and,
    from tomorrow, token_ping both call this."""
    st = gate()
    msg = st["line"]
    try:
        (notifier or _notify)(msg)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return msg


def _notify(msg):
    from scripts import notify
    notify.notify(msg)

=== single_instance.py ===
"""R-EVENING-22 A2 — a shared single-instance lock so a trading-path process (each recorder, the runner) can
never run twice. A second launch sees a LIVE lock holder and exits; a lock from a dead PID is reclaimed. The
watchdog respects these locks: it treats a lane as UP when its lock is held by a live PID, so it never spawns
a duplicate. Read-only aside from its own lock file under scheduler/.locks/."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import subprocess

_REPO = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
LOCK_DIR = os.path.join(_REPO, "scheduler", ".locks")


def _path(name):
    safe = "".join(c if c.isalnum() or c in "-_" else "_" for c in str(name))
    return os.path.join(LOCK_DIR, "%s.lock" % safe)


def _pid_alive(pid):
    try:
        r = subprocess.run(["tasklist", "/FI", "PID eq %d" % int(pid)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
        return str(int(pid)) in (r.stdout or "")
    except Exception:
        return True                                   # unknown -> assume alive (never spawn a duplicate)


def holder(name):
    """The live PID holding this lock, or None. A stale lock (dead PID / unreadable) reads as None."""
    p = _path(name)
    try:
        pid = int(open(p, encoding="utf-8").read().strip() or "0")
    except Exception:
        return None
    if pid and _pid_alive(pid):
        return pid
    return None


def is_held(name):
    """True if a LIVE process (other than this one) holds the lock."""
    h = holder(name)
    return bool(h) and h != os.getpid()


def acquire(name):
    """Take the lock for this process. Returns True if we hold it, False if a LIVE other holder exists (caller
    should exit). Never blocks; a lock error never stops the only instance."""
    try:
        os.makedirs(LOCK_DIR, exist_ok=True)
        h = holder(name)
        if h and h != os.getpid():
            return False
        with open(_path(name), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(str(os.getpid()))
        return True
    except Exception:
        return True


def release(name):
    try:
        p = _path(name)
        if holder(name) in (os.getpid(), None):
            os.remove(p)
    except Exception:
        pass


def guard(name):
    """Convenience for a record-loop entry: acquire or return False to exit. Prints a one-line reason."""
    if not acquire(name):
        print("%s: another instance already running — exiting (single-instance lock)." % name)
        return False
    return True


def _demo():
    import tempfile
    global LOCK_DIR, _pid_alive
    saved, saved_alive = LOCK_DIR, _pid_alive
    LOCK_DIR = os.path.join(tempfile.gettempdir(), "si_demo_%d" % os.getpid())
    try:
        assert acquire("E13-depth") is True            # first take succeeds
        assert holder("E13-depth") == os.getpid()
        assert is_held("E13-depth") is False           # held by US, not "another"
        # a FOREIGN live PID blocks acquire (mock aliveness so the test is deterministic)
        _pid_alive = lambda pid: True
        with open(_path("E13-depth"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("424242")
        assert is_held("E13-depth") is True
        assert acquire("E13-depth") is False           # another live holder -> we must exit
        # a DEAD PID is reclaimed
        _pid_alive = lambda pid: False
        with open(_path("E7-x"), "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("424242")
        assert holder("E7-x") is None and acquire("E7-x") is True
        print("single_instance demo OK: acquire / foreign-holder blocks / stale reclaimed.")
    finally:
        LOCK_DIR, _pid_alive = saved, saved_alive


if __name__ == "__main__":
    _demo()

_LOCK = os.path.join(ds._REPO, "scheduler", ".book20_runner.lock")


def _take_single_instance_lock():
    """R-DEDUPE / single-instance: refuse to run if another book20_runner is already looping. Writes this
    process's PID; a stale lock (PID gone) is reclaimed. Returns True if we hold the lock, False to exit."""
    try:
        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(_LOCK), exist_ok=True)
        if os.path.exists(_LOCK):
            try:
                pid = int(open(_LOCK, encoding="utf-8").read().strip() or "0")
            except Exception:
                pid = 0
            if pid and pid != os.getpid() and _pid_alive(pid):
                return False                          # another live runner holds it -> we exit
        with open(_LOCK, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(str(os.getpid()))
        return True
    except Exception:
        return True                                   # never block the only runner on a lock error


def _pid_alive(pid):
    try:
        import subprocess
        r = subprocess.run(["tasklist", "/FI", "PID eq %d" % pid], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15)
        return str(pid) in (r.stdout or "")
    except Exception:
        return True                                   # unknown -> assume alive (never spawn a duplicate)


def poll_loop(poll_secs=120, max_polls=None):
    """The session poll used by the post-login chain. Re-checks gate.order_ok on EVERY poll (fail-closed).
    Detached, read-only market data, paper only. Exits at the close or after max_polls. Single-instance:
    exits immediately if another runner already holds the lock (R-DEDUPE)."""
    import time
    from engine1_rebuild import scan
    if not _take_single_instance_lock():
        print("book20_runner: another instance is already running — exiting (single-instance lock).")
        return
    n = 0
    while True:
        scan.write_heartbeat("book20", "20-day book poll")
        run_once(write=True)
        n += 1
        if max_polls and n >= max_polls:
            break
        time.sleep(poll_secs)

    so a demo can never touch a live ledger (the instrument-111 leak). Restores the real paths afterwards."""

RETIRED = {"run_live.py"}


def _expected_jobs():
    """Parse START_E1_ALL.bat for the `pythonw scripts\\X.py [args]` jobs it launches. Skips REM-commented
    lines (a commented job is not a job) and RETIRED scripts (never resurrected)."""
    import re as _re
    jobs = []
    try:
        for ln in open(START_BAT, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"):
            if ln.lstrip().upper().startswith("REM"):
                continue                                     # R-AUDIT-DAY1-FIX (2): a commented line is not a job
            m = _re.search(r"pythonw\s+(scripts\\[\w.\-]+\.py)([^\r\n]*)", ln)
            if m and os.path.basename(m.group(1).strip()) not in RETIRED:
                jobs.append((os.path.basename(m.group(1).strip()), m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip()))
    except Exception:
        pass
    return jobs


def _job_running(script_base):
    """True if a pythonw process is running that script (fail-safe True on error — never spam launches)."""
    try:
        out = subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-Command",
                              "(Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name='pythonw.exe'\" | "
                              "Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -like '*%s*' } | Measure-Object).Count" % script_base],
                             capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=15,
                             creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW).stdout.strip()
        # Fail-safe against DUPLICATE launches (R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2): only an explicit "0" means not running;
        # an EMPTY result (the CIM query timing out under heavy backtest load) is assumed RUNNING, never
        # dead — otherwise the watchdog spawns a second copy every time the query is slow.
        return out != "0"
    except Exception:
        return True


def _launch_job(script, args):
    argl = "'%s'" % script + ("," + ",".join("'%s'" % a for a in args.split()) if args.strip() else "")
    ps = ("Start-Process pythonw -WindowStyle Hidden -ArgumentList %s -WorkingDirectory '%s'"
          % (argl, ds._REPO))
    try:
        from scripts.memory_guard import log_launch
        log_launch(os.path.basename(script), "watchdog RESURRECT (hidden, pythonw)")   # R-RESTART-RESUME (2)
    except Exception:
        pass
    try:
        subprocess.run(["powershell", "-NoProfile", "-ExecutionPolicy", "Bypass", "-Command", ps],
                       timeout=30, capture_output=True, creationflags=CREATE_NO_WINDOW)
    except Exception:
        pass

def _pythonw():
    """R-HIDE-WINDOWS: the windowless interpreter (pythonw.exe) so a launched job NEVER pops a console."""
    exe = sys.executable
    cand = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(exe), "pythonw.exe")
    return cand if os.path.exists(cand) else exe

tool_result: CRASH_LOOP_N = 3          # R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2: this many restarts within the window = a crash loop
CRASH_LOOP_WIN = 30.0     # minutes
QUARANTINE_MIN = 120.0    # quarantine a crash-looping job for this long, then retry once


def resurrect_jobs(now, launch=True, notify_fn=None):
    """R-E1-RESURRECT: restart ANY START_E1_ALL job (run_live, run_evening, …) that is not running; log
    it; and flag it so the next ⏱ reports it. Per-job cooldown so a crash-looping job is not relaunched
    every cycle. Never restarts the watchdog itself. Returns the list restarted this pass.
    R-E1-FIT-IN-RAM: while jobs are paused for a backtest, resurrect nothing."""
    if _jobs_paused():
        return []
    st = _read(STATE_FILE) or {}
    cool = st.get("job_restart_cooldown", {})
    hist = st.get("job_restart_history", {})          # R-E1-FINAL-ARCH C2: crash-loop backoff
    quar = st.get("job_quarantine", {})
    restarted, newly_quarantined = [], []
    for base, script, args in _expected_jobs():
        if base == "watchdog.py" or base in ONE_SHOT or base in RETIRED:
            continue                 # never resurrect the watchdog, a finished one-shot, or a RETIRED job (run_live)
        if base == SWEEP_BASE and _sweep_held(now):
            continue                 # R-E1-SWEEP-PAUSE: sweep held until its resume_at
        if (now - quar.get(base, 0)) / 60.0 < QUARANTINE_MIN:
            continue                 # crash-looping job is quarantined — do not thrash it
        if _job_running(base):
            continue
        if (now - cool.get(base, 0)) / 60.0 < JOB_COOLDOWN_MIN:
            continue
        # crash-loop detection: if it needed CRASH_LOOP_N restarts within CRASH_LOOP_WIN, it is missing a
        # prerequisite (token / market data / etc.) — quarantine it and ring ONCE, do not restart forever.
        h = [t for t in hist.get(base, []) if (now - t) / 60.0 < CRASH_LOOP_WIN] + [now]
        hist[base] = h[-CRASH_LOOP_N:]
        if len(h) >= CRASH_LOOP_N:
            quar[base] = now
            newly_quarantined.append(base)
            continue
        if launch:
            _launch_job(script, args)
        cool[base] = now
        restarted.append(base)
        try:
            with open(RESTART_LOG, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write("%s restarted %s\n" % (_ist().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M IST"), base))
        except Exception:
            pass
    if restarted or newly_quarantined:
        st["job_restart_cooldown"] = cool
        st["job_restart_history"] = hist
        st["job_quarantine"] = quar
        if restarted:
            st["pending_restart_report"] = restarted
            st["pending_restart_ts"] = now
        _write_state(st)
    if newly_quarantined:
        (notify_fn or notify)("\U0001f64b [E1] These jobs keep crashing on launch and are now quarantined "
                              "(no more auto-restart for %dh): %s. Likely a missing prerequisite (Kite token, "
                              "market hours, or data). Check them, then double-click START_E1_ALL.bat."
                              % (int(QUARANTINE_MIN / 60), ", ".join(newly_quarantined)))
    return restarted


def _conf_status_sig():
    """A fingerprint of the STATUS column of CONFORMANCE.md — the conforms/partial/missing/wall tokens in
    order. It changes only when a row's STATUS changes (R-E1-RING-FOREVER checklist watchdog)."""
    try:
        import hashlib
        import re as _re
        txt = open(CONF_PATH, encoding="utf-8").read()
        toks = _re.findall(r"\*\*(conforms|partial|missing|wall)\*\*", txt)
        return hashlib.md5("|".join(toks).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() if toks else None
    except Exception:
        return None

    """A build session is active if the owner-active marker is fresh (<4h), or the re-scan is not yet
    certified (build work still expected). Off-hours idle -> no nag."""

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- rework
#
# A checker's REJECTED is a judgement about the work. No medic may overturn it --
# but the chain need not hand it to a human either. It hands it back to a BUILDER
# with the checker's own findings attached, and lets the checker look again.
#
# WHY THE BRIEF CARRIES THE WHOLE FINDINGS SECTION AND NOT THE VERDICT LINE.
# Learned on ITEM 2, 2026-08-11, the expensive way. Cycle 1's brief was built from
# the one-line verdict, which named `volume`. The builder fixed volume. The next
# checker rejected it again and said why: the real defect was field-name
# INTERSECTION, which also silently drops `oi` -- "the unbuilt half of cycle 1's
# INV-COMPARED-MEANS-COMPARED, which the rework brief did not carry."
# A verdict line is a summary. The audit behind it is the actual finding, and a
# brief narrower than the audit buys a rework cycle that fixes a symptom.

REWORK_MAX = 2                    # cycles per item before a human is asked
REWORK_STATE = os.path.join(LOG_DIR, "rework_state.json")


def _rework_state():
    if not os.path.exists(REWORK_STATE):
        return {}
    try:
        with open(REWORK_STATE, encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            return json.load(fh)
    except (ValueError, OSError):
        return {}


def rework_count(item):
    return int(_rework_state().get(str(item.num), 0))


def set_rework_count(item, n):
    st = _rework_state()
    st[str(item.num)] = n

    try:
        from data_acquisition import kite

        return True
    except Exception:

def _sleep_until(target):
    while True:
        secs = (target - _ist()).total_seconds()
        if secs <= 0:
            return
        time.sleep(min(secs, 1800))


def start_catcher(_popen=subprocess.Popen, _running=None):
    """Start the port-80 login catcher DETACHED and hidden, unless one is already up.

    Reuses launch_e1's "is this already running?" reader rather than a second opinion: a duplicate
    catcher would lose the port-80 bind and fall through to the paste gate, which is exactly the
    ceremony this ruling removes. Returns True if one was started.
    """
    from scripts import launch_e1 as L
    cmdlines = L.running_cmdlines() if _running is None else _running
    if L.is_running([CATCHER[0]], cmdlines):
        return False
    _popen([L.pythonw(), *CATCHER], cwd=REPO, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL, stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL,
           creationflags=L.NO_WINDOW | L.DETACHED)

=== STOP_E1_STACK.bat ===
@echo off
REM ===========================================================================
REM  STOP_E1_STACK.bat  (owner ruling R-NIGHT-CLOSE, 21 Sep 2026)
REM  Double-click this to STOP the whole trading-engines-v2 python stack:
REM  run_live, book20_runner, the recorders (E4 chain / E7 tick / E12 NSE-BSE /
REM  E13 depth / E14 posn), kite_catcher, token_ping, site_loop, watchdog, and any
REM  heavy scan/hunt (pair sweep, ruler factory, index tests).
REM  It leaves this session's MCP helpers (windows-mcp / uv 'serve') ALONE.
REM  Only this file and START_E1_STACK.bat may stop/start the stack (rulebook).
REM ===========================================================================
echo Stopping the E1 stack (trading-engines-v2 python processes)...
powershell -NoProfile -Command "$pat='scripts|engine1_rebuild|engine4_options|engine7_parity|engine9|engine12_xchange|engine21_overnight|engine_youtube|book20_runner|run_live'; $ps=Get-CimInstance Win32_Process -Filter \"Name like 'python%%'\" | Where-Object { $_.CommandLine -match $pat -and $_.CommandLine -notmatch 'windows-mcp|serve' }; if(-not $ps){ Write-Output 'nothing to stop.' } else { $ps | ForEach-Object { Write-Output ('  killed PID {0}: {1}' -f $_.ProcessId, ($_.CommandLine).Trim()); Stop-Process -Id $_.ProcessId -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue } }"
echo Done. Now double-click START_E1_STACK.bat to start the clean stack.
pause

@echo off
REM ===========================================================================
REM  START_E1_STACK.bat  (owner ruling R-NIGHT-CLOSE, 21 Sep 2026)
REM  Double-click this to START the clean E1 stack on the ONE correct
REM  interpreter (Python 3.10). It starts EXACTLY THREE processes, nothing else:
REM
REM   1. token_ping   scripts\token_ping.py:main  -> at 06:00 IST starts the

REM      --auto). On login landing kite_catcher._finish (kite_catcher.py:104) calls
REM      kite_login.post_login (kite_login.py:240), which arms the RECORDERS
REM      (_arm_recorder, kite_login.py:247) and the single order path
REM      BOOK20_RUNNER (_arm_book20, kite_login.py:264). Never run_live.
REM   2. watchdog     scripts\watchdog.py:main    -> keep-alive / stall summon.
REM   3. site_loop    scripts\site_loop.py:main   -> rebuild + publish the site.
